## § 0 — Intent (whose words, what outcome)

**Matt, verbatim (2026-09-20):** *"make that a fully playable sim that we can build out in an Astra 2D scene with our 2D animated character with monsters having animation placeholders and the player character and monsters having placeholder VFX. I want to play the game and compare how accurately we can get it to my video of playing the game in Grim Dawn on the EOR warlord."*

**Terminal artifact:** a **desktop build Matt can launch** — Crucible waves 150–160, the Keeper as the EoR warlord, GD-faithful mouse binds, geometry-true placeholders — sitting on a **headless GDScript runtime whose fidelity to the Python oracle is already graded (T-A)** and which writes the telemetry from which his play is compared to his footage (T-B). **The autonomous run ENDS at the handoff.** T-B play sessions, the feel round and T-C side-by-side need Matt's hands and eye; they are HITL sessions after the run, never inside it.

## § 2 — The central design: ONE runtime, TWO configurations, a DIVERGENCE REGISTER

The P0 returns show the oracle and the thing Matt wants to play are *deliberately not the same fight* (walls ring vs circle, pools 0.0 vs D-LIFT, 0.15 roll vs the flag). The run holds them apart by construction:

- **`ORACLE` config** — the fold/limb set of record (v3.2 **V0**), exactly as the sealed cell ran: wall = `CIRCLE R = 43.758… m`, pool magnitude 0.0, uniform 0.15 cast-interrupt roll, fire-time damage resolution, summons leader-coincident/positionless/immortal, no player crit, scripted pilot (PM1 + M-POL-2 channel policy). **T-A is graded under this config and no other.**
- **`PLAY` config** — what Matt plays: video-measured ring at a registered `u`, pools per **D-LIFT-1/2**, the **`interrupts_channel` flag** (D-CP2-2), human input.
- **The DIVERGENCE REGISTER** — every switch by which `PLAY` differs from `ORACLE` is one enumerated row (id, ORACLE value, PLAY value, authority, how it is tested since T-A cannot grade it). The config id + register hash are stamped into every telemetry file. **A T-B miss is then attributable to exactly one of FIVE classes: port (bounded by T-A) · declared divergence · model gap · pilot · placeholder/instrument** (WARN-2 — a footage instrument and the recorder can disagree about the *same* fight; every T-B statistic is computed on the denominator **as the footage instrument defines it**, stated beside the row). A difference that is in none of the five is a defect in the register.

**Conductor rulings made at charter (veto-open — Matt's one word reverses any):**
- **R-KP-0a · Damage resolves at FIRE TIME in both configs for this lap.** Strafing does not dodge (census § 1.3: there is no arrival hit-test in the oracle, and an arrival rule is undecoded — Law 3). It is divergence-register row 0 *as a non-divergence carried with its confound named*; the arrival hit-test is next-lap, behind sibling S1. *Player consequence:* projectiles you side-step will still land. The referent sat at full health 42.84 % of the fight; he was not dodging for a living.
- **R-KP-0b · Summons in PLAY keep the oracle's law** (coincident, immortal, basics-only); the view draws them beside the player as a registered presentation choice.
- **R-KP-0c · The arena is a swappable data object**; the wall-less hull is retired as a scale-pin target; `u` for PLAY is a registered runtime choice inside `[0.22277, 0.3663]`, rider routed to galadriel. A tighter walled arena than the sealed spread is a **declared expectation**.
- **R-KP-0e · Bound skills the oracle never casts (census (c-6); WARN-1).** Blitz is not simulated at all; Vire's Might and Rune of Rush are bare displacements; only War Cry has a modelled effect. **PLAY builds them from the pack's own geometry where the pack carries the value** (Blitz 3 targets / 180° / 300 % run speed; Vire's Might path radius 2.2 × end 1.5; War Cry radius 16.0) as **declared runtime additions — divergence-register rows, OFF under `ORACLE`**; any rider the pack does not carry is **PLACEHOLDER-INERT, named on the HUD tooltip and in the handoff**, never invented (Law 3). *Player consequence:* three of your four keys do less than they did in the video, and the build tells you which and why.
- **R-KP-0f · The Vanguard Banner (census (c-10); WARN-11)** — ~~×2.0~~ **×1.0319** *(magnitude corrected at KP-7: the +100 % is additive onto a sheet already at +3036 %)* player damage inside an 8 m aura, per-tick, no hysteresis — is **MODEL-BOUND regardless of its Tier-2 lift status**: drawn on the plate at true radius, a HUD buff icon that drops the instant you leave, and a § 4.4 probe row. Its *placement* is DECLARED-not-decoded and is a divergence-register row.
- **R-KP-0d · Census hazard (c-2)** (pack says monster-side mitigation unmodelled; oracle applies it per body) is NOT adjudicated by the conductor — gamora runs her named one-record refuting test as the first act of W1, and V6 is written to its answer.

## § 3 — Fit test (desirable-run-pattern § 3)

- **F1 Enumerable? YES** — § 1: ~~72~~ **89** census row ids (KP-9), 22 lift items, R-1…R-9 + R2D-1…10, 11 scene gaps, 3 sealed cells, 1 geometry file.
- **F2 Decidable? YES** — § 4 evaluates without Matt; every judgment-shaped doneness (feel, "looks like GD") is converted to the post-run HITL boundary, not left inside the run.
- **F3 Pre-drainable? YES — drained:** Q81 F1–F7, Q66, D-CP2-1..3, D-LIFT-1/2, R-KP-0a..d. Residual forks are reasoning-boundaries (lift-row shape, module layout, token look). **One residual Matt item does not block launch:** P0-b OQ-1 (held-RMB auto-resume) — see § 6.
- **F4 Authority-resident? YES** — the conductor authored SKIRT, F-5 and the 2D delta and holds the KC2 lineage. ⚠ `SPEC-AUTHOR → DRIFT-CRITIC` applies at every fold judged against those specs; jack-ryan Gate-2 is the independent check.

## § 4 — Decidable target-state (the run is DONE when every row evaluates, without Matt)

1. **baton-v3.2 CUT** — Tier 1 V0–V9 each **LIFTED** (rows-not-fields, `(value, scope, provenance)` triples, DR-1/2/3) **or HONEST-FAIL** as a named absence with pin + what-was-searched; Tier 2 likewise; census PRE == POST; digests derived; v3.1 superseded FORWARD (additive — every v3.1 member path survives). jack-ryan Gate-2: no BLOCK outstanding.
3. **COVERAGE GATE — before T-A, and printed beside every band (BLOCK-B; pattern § 6.1, paid for by KIT-FIDELITY).** All ~~72~~ **89** *(KP-9; jack-ryan W1 pre-read BLOCK-1 propagation)* census rows mapped *mechanically* (a table emitted by the runtime's own rule census, not prose) to exactly one of **IMPLEMENTED · DIVERGENCE(row id) · RUNTIME-CHOICE(absent_ref) · OUT-OF-SCOPE(§ 1 citation)**; counts sum to ~~72~~ **89**; **zero unmapped**. **T-A does not run until this gate is green; every T-A band is reported as `value @ coverage k/89`.** An accurate port of a fraction of the fight is not a fidelity result.
2. **Runtime T-0** — `reincarnated-godot/kc2_runtime/` loads v3.2 under the SKIRT § 1 gate sequence headless; **all 9 test vectors reproduced** to declared precision — ⚑ and the report says what that proves: the vectors are **emitter-restated, not sim-produced** (`ABS-TEST-VECTORS-SIM-SOURCED`), so **T-0 proves agreement with the EMITTER, not with the oracle** (WARN-3); oracle agreement is T-A's job alone; rule census + runtime-choice ledger emitted; both channel DO-NOTs mechanically enforced; **zero `Input`, zero `res://` art, zero scene-tree dependency** asserted by scan.
4. **T-A graded** under `ORACLE` config, 5 salts per arm, against **pre-registered bands** (prereg committed ALONE before the first graded run — D4): terminal-wave 5-vector · per-wave durations · the five-state census on the **constructed denominator** · release duty / plant ratio · the deferred-arrival **conservation identity → 0** · the **inertness relations** (NULL arm ≡ base) as structural rows. **The prereg is IMMUTABLE once committed — any change is a new dated prereg version with the reason, never an edit, and a change made after a graded run exists is a HALT (WARN-16). "Two attempts" = two graded T-A runs against the same prereg version.** **The prereg (BLOCK-C) names, before any graded run: (i) row DECISIVENESS — `EXACT` rows (inertness digests, conservation identity, coverage) are pass/fail with no tolerance; `BAND` rows (terminal 5-vector at σ 2.32, per-salt uptime 0.783–0.914, release duty 0.086–0.215) are graded on gamora-derived widths and are individually NON-decisive; (ii) a FAIL TAXONOMY — `STRUCTURAL` (any EXACT row red → the port is wrong; the run does NOT proceed to W4 until repaired or HALTs after two attempts) · `STATISTICAL` (BAND rows only → finding, handoff proceeds) · `UNGRADEABLE` (instrument cannot decide → declared); (iii) the T-B QUOTING CAP as a HARNESS REFUSAL on R2D-10's pattern — the T-B grader reads the T-A verdict file and **refuses to print a fidelity figure** while T-A is absent, STRUCTURAL-red, or coverage < 89/89-mapped; it prints raw statistics only.** **jack-ryan Gate-2 sits AT THE W3 SEAL on the T-A grade and any reclassification — the fold never leaves the run ungraded by an independent seat.** ⚑ Declared ceiling (WARN-14): without sibling S5 the draw-site counters show *that* streams diverge, never *where*; the T-A report says so.
5. **Scene** — own Godot project (not `cliffside_v*`); runtime vendored by sha (refuse-on-mismatch); headless probes green: **geometry-true** (every MODEL-BOUND dimension of every VFX/telegraph asserted against pack values) · **no `CollisionObject2D` under any token** · **no combat duration handed to a 60-Hz grammar** · the three arena traps by assertion · **summon tokens carry no health bar and no `hp` field in the view (WARN-13)** · Camera2D zoom gate · recorder writes one tick-stamped stream, config id + register hash in the header.
6. **Desktop build** launches on the Mac (macOS preset, unsigned local `.app`); a scripted 60-s smoke session produces a valid telemetry file. **Fallback:** the Godot editor on Matt's Mac for feel-only sessions — never a Web build.
7. **Handoff packet** — build + one-page how-to-play (binds) + the divergence register + the T-A report + the T-B grader ready to run on his first session + findings harvest. Gate-2 on the whole.

**HALT-to-Matt boundaries:** **committed-truth conflict — a decisions-log or Matt-ruled contradiction found in substrate HALTs the affected piece (pattern § 4, verbatim class; three live contradictions already sit in this substrate: the pack's stale `runtime_containment` row, the pack-vs-oracle mitigation claim (c-2), the geometry file's superseded `u`)** · **disk free < 40 GB (81 GB at charter)** · **conductor compaction → charter-freshness gate from disk + re-read of this charter and ledger BEFORE any routing** · **any seat write outside its named tree (`kc2_runtime/` · `astra_test_01/kc2play/` · its own notes · the engine paths its brief names)** · any product-seam commitment · charter amendment · jack-ryan BLOCK · any result that would move a sealed grade of record (K-7) · taste/naming · external-state danger (disk, usage, credentials) · two consecutive failed attempts at the same gate by the same seat.

### § 6.1 — ⚑ G-IMG · THE FIRST-IMAGE GATE (Matt-added at launch, KP-3 — a commitment-boundary HALT)

**Matt verbatim:** *"let's add one gate on the very first image. I want to see it and understand the planned burst before the first burst fires."*
**No Astra/codex burst of this run fires — including the § 7.3 feasibility probe — until Matt has (a) SEEN the first frame and (b) read the plain-language plan for the first burst, and given his word.** Because the image must precede every burst, it is a **labelled MOCK** composed without a burst from first-party assets + true geometry (drax, W1): 1920×1080 at `ZOOM-GD`, the video-measured ring + 4 islands + 6 pools projected under the § 1 law, the Banner aura at 8 m, the Keeper idle cell at 86.6 px, monster tokens at model radius, the EoR ring at 227.0 × 181.2 px radii, a HUD wireframe. Every dimension computed from the formula; the mock says MOCK on its face. The burst plan (conductor-authored): what the burst is asked to make, from which references, under which caps, what comes back, what is checked, what could go wrong, what happens next. Delivered to the Desktop packet AND Matt's phone. **Everything not a burst proceeds meanwhile** (W1 seats, the v3.2 cut, drax's runtime). Matt's eye on the mock is a legitimate source of scene rulings; they land in this ledger as class `matt`.

## § 7 — Standing laws carried in (cite-by-name)

1. **KC2 lineage:** K-7 · Law 3 (no fitted constants, no invented rules) · GL-6/7/10/12/13 · DR-1/2/3 · D4 prereg-alone · bands-not-tape (R-L91-4) · ship-ratios (D-MPOL2-2) · G5 = 0.0960 · digests derived-never-retyped · D-LIFT layer law · close-out-derived-from-ledger · corrigenda-forward · OP § 4.11 value-set sweep at every value-changing fold.
2. **From P0-c (architect pass § 6.5):** generated projects are artifacts, never edited · exclusion of the grammars' hit resolver **by assertion** · the sim starts and cancels every combat-timed effect · runtime home `kc2_runtime/`, headless `--script` runners.
3. **Concurrency with Runs C-6 / C-7** (they own the burst lane's TOOLING + freeze): KC2-PLAY **writes nothing under `astra_test_01/burst/`**; its scene tree is `astra_test_01/kc2play/`, consuming the burst exporter read-only at a **pinned MANIFEST sha**; all heavy work (headless Godot, suites) under `~/astra-burst/.heavy.lock` via `heavy_lock.py`; a needed change *inside* `burst/` is a **request to the C-7 conductor**, never a write. *First W2 act is a feasibility probe of this arrangement; if the wrapper cannot run a burst outside `burst/`, the scene seat falls to drax (L3's fallback) and that is a finding, not a halt.* **The fallback is PRICED (WARN-7): drax would then hold runtime + vendor + preset + scene. Pre-registered SHED LIST, in order, if that seat saturates: (1) damage numbers · (2) buff row beyond the Banner icon · (3) proc floating labels → a single proc ticker · (4) the house-zoom toggle. Never shed: recorder, coverage table, geometry-true probes, the Banner aura, the restart key.**
4. **Figure hygiene:** no T-B figure is quoted as fidelity until T-A is graded (Q81-F7); HP expected shape is *mostly-full, shallow fast saw-tooth, rare deep excursions* — never "living on leech."

NO SEAM FILLS A GAP ON ITS OWN AUTHORITY.** ⚑⚑ **AND HIS *WHY* RETROACTIVELY JUSTIFIES THE WHOLE DISCIPLINE:** *"a line in the sand that we have a referent battle system so that we can decide to tweak it before joining other kits into it and eventually other games, so that this game becomes a JOIN KEY and also its own game."* ⚑ **A fitted constant is a lie you can only tell ONCE — it encodes this game's data in a form nobody can invert, so it cannot be transported to a second kit or a second game. **GD's combat math is twelve years of Diablo-2-lineage accretion (conversion chains, RR stacking, flat-vs-percent ordering) and what makes it an excellent REFERENT — real, complete, measurable — is not what makes it a good FOUNDATION.** *He has already named the guard ("decide to tweak it before joining other kits"); the discipline that enforces it is to **write down which parts we intend to KEEP and which we reproduce only to prove we understood them — NOW, while the referent is being built.*** Precedent: **D3 shipped an itemization faithful to a spec nobody had stress-tested against play — fidelity to the SPEC, not the EXPERIENCE**; PoE's manifesto tradition exists precisely to keep deliberateness explicit rather than let complexity read as intent. ⚑ **(2) A JOIN KEY NEEDS A SCHEMA, NOT A CORRECT INSTANCE** — one character, one build, one game; **the v3.x pack shape is GRIM-DAWN-SHAPED** and nothing has tested whether its concepts survive a game with no energy, three resources, or cooldowns instead of a resource. *Better asked while the second kit is hypothetical than after it has been forced into a schema built for the first.* **STANDING QUEUE OPENED WITH SIX ENTRIES SO THE POLICY HAS A POPULATION ON ITS FIRST DAY:** **C-1** off-channel recovery rate (no row/no grade; bound credited as realized where the sibling row distinguishes them) · **C-5** the Guardian's `IMPL` cell (0 casts / 2,567 UNDECIDED) · **C-6** `OPEN-UNKILLABLE` (~30×). |

UNFUN IS A REBUILD.** A messy system people love can be documented, refactored and re-exposed; **a clean dead one cannot have fun ADDED to it.** ⚑⚑ **AND MY OWN D3 CITATION WAS EVIDENCE FOR HIM, DEPLOYED AGAINST HIM:** D3's launch itemization **was** the concept piece — smart drops, clean stat budgets, a designed economy — faithful to a spec, internally coherent, joyless; every honestly-declared absence is a place we know not to trust a tweak. **The Commission Rule converts *opaque but fun* into *opaque, fun, and STEERABLE*** — without anyone ever having to guess which of the three they hold. When that hand chooses, it is loose; Type-B is not loose. The conclusion holds as the better-supported reading, not as a proof — which is why L5 is live.** ⚑ The testimony leg below is the WEAK one and is kept as corroboration only: *not noticing* a break is fully compatible with a reflexive re-press (reflexive repair is a mechanism for not noticing). ⚑ And the asymmetry is named: the same sentence is ACCEPTED in its first clause and OVERRULED in its second (his reason was energy; the measured trigger was the wave transition) — both on measurement, neither on preference. *Original text, kept as lineage:* **Matt verbatim:** *"I don't recall anything interrupting the spin. I believe it was continuous, except for when I stopped it, thinking I might save some mana/energy."* **Reading, per the L-84 discipline (testimony is a hypothesis source; a measurement refutes an execution, never the testimony):** the footage MEASURES eight ≈0.60 s Type-B breaks (5 Blitz, 3 Vire's Might; range 0.53–0.67 s) — and the pilot did not perceive one of them. **A break the hand never noticed is a break the hand never repaired: he did not re-press.** → **OQ-1 RULED: Type-B releases AUTO-RESUME while RMB remains held** (the 0.60 s is a mechanical lockout the game serves and ends by itself). His second clause names the OTHER population: deliberate releases — **Type-A is the hand letting go; it resumes only on input.** ⚑ Two things this testimony does NOT do, recorded so nobody does them later: (i) it does not delete the Type-B population — the breaks are measured and the `interrupts_channel` flag (D-CP2-2) stands; (ii) it does not revive `energy_gated_release` — his *reason* was energy, the *measured trigger* was the wave transition (every release began ≥ 0.846 of ceiling; H-MC-1 stays REFUTED on its trigger). *He was not counting his energy; he was counting the fight* — and believing he was counting his energy. The runtime implements the measurement; the HUD will let him see which it was. |
